SNAPSHOT IGNORE POLICY
============================================================

This file is rebuilt from the current Cinerific workspace every time ./read_project.sh runs.
Ignored paths are omitted from the project tree and file dumps unless they appear inside an included text file such as .gitignore or read_project.sh.

The snapshot intentionally ignores:
- .git
- .gradle
- .idea
- app/build
- build
- .kotlin
- .tmp
- .cache
- .DS_Store
- local.properties

- *.jks
- *.keystore

Binary image and screenshot files are inventoried by path, size, and modified time only. Raw binary bytes are not printed.

     1	package com.prahlin.cinerific
     2	
     3	import android.app.UiModeManager
     4	import android.graphics.Color
     5	import android.os.Build
     6	import android.os.Bundle
     7	import android.os.SystemClock
     8	import androidx.activity.ComponentActivity
     9	import androidx.activity.SystemBarStyle
    10	import androidx.activity.compose.setContent
    11	import androidx.activity.enableEdgeToEdge
    12	import androidx.core.view.WindowCompat
    13	import com.prahlin.cinerific.ui.CinerificApp
    14	import com.prahlin.cinerific.ui.theme.CinerificTheme
    15	
    16	class MainActivity : ComponentActivity() {
    17	    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
    18	        val bootStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
    19	        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S) {
    20	            getSystemService(UiModeManager::class.java).setApplicationNightMode(
    21	                UiModeManager.MODE_NIGHT_YES
    22	            )
    23	        }
    24	        enableEdgeToEdge(
    25	            statusBarStyle = SystemBarStyle.dark(Color.TRANSPARENT),
    26	            navigationBarStyle = SystemBarStyle.dark(Color.BLACK),
    27	        )
    28	        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
    29	        window.statusBarColor = Color.TRANSPARENT
    30	        window.navigationBarColor = Color.BLACK
    31	        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.P) {
    32	            window.navigationBarDividerColor = Color.BLACK
    33	        }
    34	        WindowCompat.getInsetsController(window, window.decorView).apply {
    35	            isAppearanceLightStatusBars = false
    36	            isAppearanceLightNavigationBars = false
    37	        }
    38	        setContent {
    39	            CinerificTheme {
    40	                CinerificApp(bootStartMillis = bootStartMillis)
    41	            }
    42	        }
    43	    }
    44	}

   101	
   102	@Composable
   103	fun CinerificApp(bootStartMillis: Long = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()) {
   104	    var showHome by remember(bootStartMillis) { mutableStateOf(false) }
   105	    var signedInProfile by remember(bootStartMillis) { mutableStateOf(CinerificProfile.Guest) }
   106	    var selectedLanguage by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(CinerificLanguage.English) }
   107	
   108	    if (showHome) {
   109	        CinerificLocalizedResources(selectedLanguage) {
   110	            CinerificMainExperience(
   111	                signedInProfile = signedInProfile,
   112	                selectedLanguage = selectedLanguage,
   113	                onLanguageSelected = { selectedLanguage = it },
   114	                onSignOut = {
   115	                    signedInProfile = CinerificProfile.Guest
   116	                    showHome = false
   117	                }
   118	            )
   119	        }
   120	    } else {
   121	        AndroidView(
   122	            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
   123	            factory = { context ->
   124	                CinerificIntroView(context).apply {
   125	                    this.bootStartMillis = bootStartMillis
   126	                    onAvatarSelected = { profile ->
   127	                        signedInProfile = profile
   128	                        showHome = true
   129	                    }
   130	                }
   131	            },
   132	            update = { view ->
   133	                view.bootStartMillis = bootStartMillis
   134	                view.onAvatarSelected = { profile ->
   135	                    signedInProfile = profile
   136	                    showHome = true
   137	                }
   138	            }
   139	        )
   140	    }
   141	}
   142	
   143	internal enum class CinerificDestination {
   144	    Home,
   145	    Movies,
   146	    Shows,
   147	    Favorites,
   148	    Settings,
   149	    ProgramDetails
   150	}
   151	
   152	internal enum class CinerificProfile(
   153	    @DrawableRes val avatarResId: Int,
   154	    @DrawableRes val nameResId: Int
   155	) {
   156	    Steve(R.drawable.steve_avatar, R.drawable.steve_name),
   157	    Martin(R.drawable.martin_avatar, R.drawable.martin_name),
   158	    Janny(R.drawable.janny_avatar, R.drawable.janny_name),
   159	    Guest(R.drawable.guest_avatar, R.drawable.guest_name)
   160	}
   161	
   162	@Composable
   163	private fun CinerificMainExperience(
   164	    signedInProfile: CinerificProfile,
   165	    selectedLanguage: CinerificLanguage,
   166	    onLanguageSelected: (CinerificLanguage) -> Unit,
   167	    onSignOut: () -> Unit
   168	) {
   169	    var destination by remember { mutableStateOf(CinerificDestination.Home) }
   170	    var selectedProgramTitle by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf("Sink or Swim") }
   171	    var favoriteProgramTitles by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(emptyList<String>()) }
   172	    var favoritesFullPromptRequestId by remember { mutableStateOf(0) }
   173	    var autoLogoutEnabled by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(false) }
   174	    var userInitiatedPlaybackActive by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
   175	    var lastInteractionMillis by remember { mutableStateOf(SystemClock.uptimeMillis()) }
   176	    val playbackSessionController = remember(userInitiatedPlaybackActive) {
   177	        CinerificPlaybackSessionController(
   178	            isUserInitiatedPlaybackActive = userInitiatedPlaybackActive,
   179	            onUserInitiatedPlaybackStarted = {
   180	                userInitiatedPlaybackActive = true
   181	            },
   182	            finishUserInitiatedPlayback = {
   183	                userInitiatedPlaybackActive = false
   184	                lastInteractionMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   185	            }
   186	        )
   187	    }
   188	
   189	    LaunchedEffect(autoLogoutEnabled, userInitiatedPlaybackActive, lastInteractionMillis) {
   190	        if (!autoLogoutEnabled || userInitiatedPlaybackActive) return@LaunchedEffect
   191	        delay(AUTO_LOGOUT_TIMEOUT_MS)
   192	        val inactiveForMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - lastInteractionMillis
   193	        if (
   194	            autoLogoutEnabled &&
   195	            !userInitiatedPlaybackActive &&
   196	            inactiveForMillis >= AUTO_LOGOUT_TIMEOUT_MS
   197	        ) {
   198	            onSignOut()
   199	        }
   200	    }
   201	
   202	    fun showProgramDetails(title: String) {
   203	        selectedProgramTitle = title
   204	        destination = CinerificDestination.ProgramDetails
   205	    }
   206	
   207	    fun toggleFavoriteProgram(title: String) {
   208	        if (title in favoriteProgramTitles) {
   209	            favoriteProgramTitles = favoriteProgramTitles - title
   210	            return
   211	        }
   212	
   213	        val isShow = cinerificProgramIsShow(title)
   214	        val filledSlots = cinerificFilledFavoritePlaceholderCount(
   215	            titles = favoriteProgramTitles,
   216	            isShow = isShow
   217	        )
   218	        if (filledSlots >= CINERIFIC_FAVORITES_CAPACITY) {
   219	            favoritesFullPromptRequestId += 1
   220	            return
   221	        }
   222	
   223	        favoriteProgramTitles = favoriteProgramTitles + title
   224	    }
   225	
   226	    CompositionLocalProvider(LocalCinerificPlaybackSessionController provides playbackSessionController) {
   227	        Box(
   228	            modifier = Modifier
   229	                .fillMaxSize()
   230	                .pointerInput(Unit) {
   231	                    awaitPointerEventScope {
   232	                        while (true) {
   233	                            awaitPointerEvent(PointerEventPass.Initial)
   234	                            lastInteractionMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   235	                        }
   236	                    }
   237	                }
   238	        ) {
   239	            when (destination) {
   240	                CinerificDestination.Home -> CinerificHomeScreen(
   241	                    onProgramSelected = ::showProgramDetails,
   242	                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   243	                )
   244	                CinerificDestination.Movies,
   245	                CinerificDestination.Shows,
   246	                CinerificDestination.Favorites,
   247	                CinerificDestination.Settings -> CinerificDestinationScreen(
   248	                    destination = destination,
   249	                    signedInProfile = signedInProfile,
   250	                    selectedLanguage = selectedLanguage,
   251	                    onLanguageSelected = onLanguageSelected,
   252	                    autoLogoutEnabled = autoLogoutEnabled,
   253	                    onAutoLogoutEnabledChange = { enabled ->
   254	                        autoLogoutEnabled = enabled
   255	                        lastInteractionMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   256	                    },
   257	                    onSignOut = onSignOut,
   258	                    favoriteProgramTitles = favoriteProgramTitles,
   259	                    onFavoriteToggled = ::toggleFavoriteProgram,
   260	                    onProgramSelected = ::showProgramDetails,
   261	                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   262	                )
   263	                CinerificDestination.ProgramDetails -> CinerificProgramDetailsScreen(
   264	                    programTitle = selectedProgramTitle,
   265	                    favoriteProgramTitles = favoriteProgramTitles,
   266	                    onFavoriteToggled = ::toggleFavoriteProgram,
   267	                    onProgramSelected = ::showProgramDetails,
   268	                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   269	                )
   270	            }
   271	
   272	            CinerificRightSideNavBar(
   273	                currentDestination = destination,
   274	                onDestinationSelected = { destination = it },
   275	                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   276	            )
   277	
   278	            FavoritesFullPromptOverlay(
   279	                requestId = favoritesFullPromptRequestId,
   280	                suppressed = userInitiatedPlaybackActive,
   281	                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   282	            )
   283	        }
   284	    }
   285	}
   286	
   287	@Composable
   288	private fun FavoritesFullPromptOverlay(
   289	    requestId: Int,
   290	    suppressed: Boolean,
   291	    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
   292	) {
   293	    var visible by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
   294	
   295	    LaunchedEffect(requestId, suppressed) {
   296	        if (suppressed) {
   297	            visible = false
   298	            return@LaunchedEffect
   299	        }
   300	        if (requestId == 0) {
   301	            visible = false
   302	            return@LaunchedEffect
   303	        }
   304	        visible = true
   305	        delay(FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_VISIBLE_MS)
   306	        visible = false
   307	    }
   308	
   309	    val alpha by animateFloatAsState(
   310	        targetValue = if (visible) 1f else 0f,
   311	        animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = if (visible) 140 else 190),
   312	        label = "favorites-full-prompt-alpha"
   313	    )
   314	    val promptScale by animateFloatAsState(
   315	        targetValue = if (visible) 1f else 0.94f,
   316	        animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = if (visible) 140 else 190),
   317	        label = "favorites-full-prompt-scale"
   318	    )
   319	
   320	    if (suppressed || requestId == 0 || (!visible && alpha <= 0.01f)) return
   321	
   322	    BoxWithConstraints(modifier = modifier) {
   323	        val stageScale = maxWidth.value / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH
   324	        FavoritesFullPromptBubble(
   325	            stageScale = stageScale,
   326	            modifier = Modifier
   327	                .absoluteOffset(
   328	                    x = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_X * stageScale).dp,
   329	                    y = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_Y * stageScale).dp
   330	                )
   331	                .graphicsLayer {
   332	                    this.alpha = alpha
   333	                    scaleX = promptScale
   334	                    scaleY = promptScale
   335	                    transformOrigin = TransformOrigin(0.5f, 0f)
   336	                }
   337	        )
   338	    }
   339	}
   340	
   341	@Composable
   342	private fun FavoritesFullPromptBubble(
   343	    stageScale: Float,
   344	    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
   345	) {
   346	    Box(
   347	        modifier = modifier.requiredSize(
   348	            width = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_WIDTH * stageScale).dp,
   349	            height = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_HEIGHT * stageScale).dp
   350	        )
   351	    ) {
   352	        Canvas(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
   353	            val xScale = size.width / FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_WIDTH
   354	            val yScale = size.height / FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_HEIGHT
   355	            val pointer = Path().apply {
   356	                moveTo(47.5f * xScale, 0f)
   357	                lineTo(63f * xScale, FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_TOP * yScale)
   358	                lineTo(32f * xScale, FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_TOP * yScale)
   359	                close()
   360	            }
   361	            drawPath(pointer, ColorFavoritesFullPromptAccent)
   362	            drawRoundRect(
   363	                color = ColorFavoritesFullPromptFill,
   364	                topLeft = Offset(0f, FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_TOP * yScale),
   365	                size = Size(
   366	                    width = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_WIDTH * xScale,
   367	                    height = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_HEIGHT * yScale
   368	                ),
   369	                cornerRadius = CornerRadius(
   370	                    x = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_RADIUS * xScale,
   371	                    y = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_RADIUS * yScale
   372	                )
   373	            )
   374	            drawRoundRect(
   375	                color = ColorFavoritesFullPromptAccent,
   376	                topLeft = Offset(
   377	                    x = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE * xScale / 2f,
   378	                    y = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_TOP * yScale +
   379	                        FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE * yScale / 2f
   380	                ),
   381	                size = Size(
   382	                    width = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_WIDTH - FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE) * xScale,
   383	                    height = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_HEIGHT - FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE) * yScale
   384	                ),
   385	                cornerRadius = CornerRadius(
   386	                    x = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_RADIUS * xScale,
   387	                    y = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_RADIUS * yScale
   388	                ),
   389	                style = Stroke(width = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE * min(xScale, yScale))
   390	            )
   391	        }
   392	        Text(
   393	            text = "You've\nfilled\nup on\nfavorites!",
   394	            color = ColorFavoritesFullPromptAccent,
   395	            fontFamily = CinerificAppTextFontFamily,
   396	            fontSize = (14f * stageScale).sp,
   397	            fontWeight = FontWeight.Black,
   398	            lineHeight = (21.252f * stageScale).sp,
   399	            textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
   400	            modifier = Modifier
   401	                .absoluteOffset(
   402	                    x = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_TEXT_X * stageScale).dp,
   403	                    y = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_TEXT_Y * stageScale).dp
   404	                )
   405	                .requiredSize(
   406	                    width = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_TEXT_WIDTH * stageScale).dp,
   407	                    height = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_TEXT_HEIGHT * stageScale).dp
   408	                )
   409	        )
   410	    }
   411	}
   412	
   413	@Composable
   414	private fun BootIntroFromFigma(progress: Float) {
   415	    val p = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
   416	    val images = rememberIntroImages()
   417	    val blackToBurgundy = linearSegmentMs(p, 0, SCREEN_RED_MS)
   418	    val burgundyToSettle = easedSegmentMs(p, 2500, 3900)
   419	    val background = introBackgroundBrush(
   420	        solidProgress = blackToBurgundy,
   421	        gradientProgress = burgundyToSettle
   422	    )
   423	    val logoAlpha = linearSegmentMs(p, 90, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS)
   424	    val logoEntryProgress = bouncySegmentMs(p, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS, LOGO_ENTRY_END_MS)
   425	    val logoFinalProgress = easedSegmentMs(p, FINAL_SETTLE_START_MS, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
   426	    val avatarSettleAlpha = easedSegmentMs(p, 2300, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
   427	    val avatarYOffset = lerpFloat(56f, 0f, avatarSettleAlpha)
   428	
   429	    Canvas(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
   430	        val stageScale = min(size.width / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, size.height / FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT)
   431	        val stageLeft = (size.width - FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stageScale) / 2f
   432	        val stageTop = (size.height - FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT * stageScale) / 2f
   433	
   434	        drawRect(brush = background, size = size)
   435	
   436	        if (logoAlpha > 0.01f) {
   437	            val baseBounds = FigmaBounds(x = 222f, y = 150f, w = 750f, h = 535f)
   438	            val simpleEntry = lerpBounds(
   439	                start = FigmaBounds(x = 447f, y = -417f, w = 300f, h = 214f),
   440	                end = baseBounds,
   441	                amount = logoEntryProgress
   442	            )
   443	            val eyesEntry = lerpBounds(
   444	                start = FigmaBounds(x = 453f, y = 1037f, w = 300f, h = 214f),
   445	                end = baseBounds,
   446	                amount = logoEntryProgress
   447	            )
   448	            val finalBounds = FigmaBounds(x = 297f, y = 11f, w = 600f, h = 428f)
   449	
   450	            drawFigmaImage(
   451	                image = images.logoSimple,
   452	                bounds = lerpBounds(simpleEntry, finalBounds, logoFinalProgress),
   453	                stageLeft = stageLeft,
   454	                stageTop = stageTop,
   455	                stageScale = stageScale,
   456	                alpha = logoAlpha
   457	            )
   458	            drawFigmaImage(
   459	                image = images.logoEyes,
   460	                bounds = lerpBounds(eyesEntry, finalBounds, logoFinalProgress),
   461	                stageLeft = stageLeft,
   462	                stageTop = stageTop,
   463	                stageScale = stageScale,
   464	                alpha = logoAlpha
   465	            )
   466	        }
   467	
   468	        if (avatarSettleAlpha > 0.01f) {
   469	            val y = avatarYOffset
   470	            drawFigmaImage(images.steveAvatar, FigmaBounds(130f, 428f + y, 220f, 220f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha, clipCircle = true)
   471	            drawFigmaImage(images.martinAvatar, FigmaBounds(368f, 428f + y, 220f, 220f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha, clipCircle = true)
   472	            drawFigmaImage(images.jannyAvatar, FigmaBounds(606f, 432f + y, 220f, 220f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha, clipCircle = true)
   473	            drawFigmaImage(images.guestAvatar, FigmaBounds(844f, 428f + y, 220f, 220f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha, clipCircle = true)
   474	
   475	            drawFigmaImage(images.steveName, FigmaBounds(130f, 683f + y, 220f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha)
   476	            drawFigmaImage(images.martinName, FigmaBounds(368f, 683f + y, 220f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha)
   477	            drawFigmaImage(images.jannyName, FigmaBounds(606f, 683f + y, 220f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha)
   478	            drawFigmaImage(images.guestName, FigmaBounds(854f, 683f + y, 200f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha)
   479	        }
   480	    }
   481	}

   636	private data class FigmaBounds(
   637	    val x: Float,
   638	    val y: Float,
   639	    val w: Float,
   640	    val h: Float
   641	)
   642	
   643	private data class IntroImages(
   644	    val logoSimple: ImageBitmap,
   645	    val logoEyes: ImageBitmap,
   646	    val steveAvatar: ImageBitmap,
   647	    val martinAvatar: ImageBitmap,
   648	    val jannyAvatar: ImageBitmap,
   649	    val guestAvatar: ImageBitmap,
   650	    val steveName: ImageBitmap,
   651	    val martinName: ImageBitmap,
   652	    val jannyName: ImageBitmap,
   653	    val guestName: ImageBitmap
   654	)
   655	
   656	@Composable
   657	private fun rememberIntroImages(): IntroImages {
   658	    val resources = LocalContext.current.resources
   659	    return remember(resources) {
   660	        IntroImages(
   661	            logoSimple = BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, R.drawable.logo_simple_large).asImageBitmap(),
   662	            logoEyes = BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, R.drawable.logo_eyes_large).asImageBitmap(),
   663	            steveAvatar = BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, R.drawable.steve_avatar).asImageBitmap(),
   664	            martinAvatar = BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, R.drawable.martin_avatar).asImageBitmap(),
   665	            jannyAvatar = BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, R.drawable.janny_avatar).asImageBitmap(),
   666	            guestAvatar = BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, R.drawable.guest_avatar).asImageBitmap(),
   667	            steveName = BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, R.drawable.steve_name).asImageBitmap(),
   668	            martinName = BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, R.drawable.martin_name).asImageBitmap(),
   669	            jannyName = BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, R.drawable.janny_name).asImageBitmap(),
   670	            guestName = BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, R.drawable.guest_name).asImageBitmap()
   671	        )
   672	    }
   673	}
   674	
   675	private fun DrawScope.drawFigmaImage(
   676	    image: ImageBitmap,
   677	    bounds: FigmaBounds,
   678	    stageLeft: Float,
   679	    stageTop: Float,
   680	    stageScale: Float,
   681	    alpha: Float = 1f,
   682	    clipCircle: Boolean = false
   683	) {
   684	    val left = stageLeft + bounds.x * stageScale
   685	    val top = stageTop + bounds.y * stageScale
   686	    val width = bounds.w * stageScale
   687	    val height = bounds.h * stageScale
   688	
   689	    if (clipCircle) {
   690	        val clip = Path().apply {
   691	            addOval(Rect(left, top, left + width, top + height))
   692	        }
   693	        clipPath(clip) {
   694	            drawImageBitmap(image, left, top, width, height, alpha)
   695	        }
   696	    } else {
   697	        drawImageBitmap(image, left, top, width, height, alpha)
   698	    }
   699	}
   700	
   701	private fun DrawScope.drawImageBitmap(
   702	    image: ImageBitmap,
   703	    left: Float,
   704	    top: Float,
   705	    width: Float,
   706	    height: Float,
   707	    alpha: Float
   708	) {
   709	    drawImage(
   710	        image = image,
   711	        dstOffset = IntOffset(left.roundToInt(), top.roundToInt()),
   712	        dstSize = IntSize(width.roundToInt(), height.roundToInt()),
   713	        alpha = alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
   714	    )
   715	}
   716	
   717	@Composable
   718	private fun SettlingLogoLayer(
   719	    scale: Float,
   720	    entryProgress: Float,
   721	    finalProgress: Float,
   722	    alpha: Float
   723	) {
   724	    val baseBounds = FigmaBounds(x = 222f, y = 150f, w = 750f, h = 535f)
   725	    val simpleEntry = lerpBounds(
   726	        start = FigmaBounds(x = 447f, y = 1037f, w = 300f, h = 214f),
   727	        end = baseBounds,
   728	        amount = entryProgress
   729	    )
   730	    val eyesEntry = lerpBounds(
   731	        start = FigmaBounds(x = 453f, y = -417f, w = 300f, h = 214f),
   732	        end = baseBounds,
   733	        amount = entryProgress
   734	    )
   735	    val finalBounds = FigmaBounds(x = 297f, y = 11f, w = 600f, h = 428f)
   736	    val simpleBounds = lerpBounds(simpleEntry, finalBounds, finalProgress)
   737	    val eyesBounds = lerpBounds(eyesEntry, finalBounds, finalProgress)
   738	
   739	    TransformedFigmaAssetImage(
   740	        base = baseBounds,
   741	        target = simpleBounds,
   742	        scale = scale,
   743	        resId = R.drawable.logo_simple_large,
   744	        alpha = alpha
   745	    )
   746	    TransformedFigmaAssetImage(
   747	        base = baseBounds,
   748	        target = eyesBounds,
   749	        scale = scale,
   750	        resId = R.drawable.logo_eyes_large,
   751	        alpha = alpha
   752	    )
   753	}
   754	
   755	@Composable
   756	private fun AvatarSelectionLayer(scale: Float, alpha: Float, yOffset: Float) {
   757	    val density = LocalDensity.current.density
   758	    Box(
   759	        modifier = Modifier
   760	            .requiredSize(figma(1194f, scale), figma(834f, scale))
   761	            .graphicsLayer {
   762	                this.alpha = alpha
   763	                translationY = yOffset * scale * density
   764	            }
   765	    ) {
   766	        // Figma Logo Slide 2, Property 1=Variant2, constrained inside Sign-in Frame.
   767	        FigmaAvatarImage(x = 130f, y = 428f, w = 220f, h = 220f, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.steve_avatar)
   768	        FigmaAvatarImage(x = 368f, y = 428f, w = 220f, h = 220f, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.martin_avatar)
   769	        FigmaAvatarImage(x = 606f, y = 432f, w = 220f, h = 220f, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.janny_avatar)
   770	        FigmaAvatarImage(x = 844f, y = 428f, w = 220f, h = 220f, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.guest_avatar)
   771	
   772	        FigmaAssetImage(x = 130f, y = 683f, w = 220f, h = 72f, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.steve_name)
   773	        FigmaAssetImage(x = 368f, y = 683f, w = 220f, h = 72f, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.martin_name)
   774	        FigmaAssetImage(x = 606f, y = 683f, w = 220f, h = 72f, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.janny_name)
   775	        FigmaAssetImage(x = 854f, y = 683f, w = 200f, h = 72f, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.guest_name)
   776	    }
   777	}
   778	
   779	@Composable
   780	private fun FigmaAvatarImage(
   781	    x: Float,
   782	    y: Float,
   783	    w: Float,
   784	    h: Float,
   785	    scale: Float,
   786	    @DrawableRes resId: Int
   787	) {
   788	    Image(
   789	        painter = painterResource(id = resId),
   790	        contentDescription = null,
   791	        contentScale = ContentScale.FillBounds,
   792	        modifier = Modifier
   793	            .absoluteOffset(x = figma(x, scale), y = figma(y, scale))
   794	            .requiredSize(figma(w, scale), figma(h, scale))
   795	            .clip(CircleShape)
   796	    )
   797	}
   798	
   799	@Composable
   800	private fun LogoDot(x: Float, y: Float, scale: Float, active: Boolean) {
   801	    Box(
   802	        modifier = Modifier
   803	            .absoluteOffset(x = figma(x, scale), y = figma(y, scale))
   804	            .requiredSize(figma(50.4f, scale), figma(50.4f, scale))
   805	            .clip(CircleShape)
   806	            .background(if (active) Color(0xFFE7E7E7) else Color(0xFF050505))
   807	    )
   808	}
   809	
   810	@Composable
   811	private fun PromoBackgroundLayer(
   812	    scale: Float,
   813	    darkOverlay: Float,
   814	    @DrawableRes backgroundResId: Int,
   815	    alpha: Float = 1f
   816	) {
   817	    // Nodes 793:7049 / 793:7050 at x:0 y:0 size:1584x1584.
   818	    FigmaAssetImage(
   819	        x = 0f,
   820	        y = 0f,
   821	        w = 1584f,
   822	        h = 1584f,
   823	        scale = scale,
   824	        resId = backgroundResId,
   825	        alpha = alpha
   826	    )
   827	
   828	    if (darkOverlay > 0f) {
   829	        Box(
   830	            modifier = Modifier
   831	                .absoluteOffset(x = figma(0f, scale), y = figma(0f, scale))
   832	                .requiredSize(figma(1584f, scale), figma(1584f, scale))
   833	                .graphicsLayer(alpha = alpha)
   834	                .background(Color.Black.copy(alpha = darkOverlay))
   835	        )
   836	    }
   837	}
   838	
   839	@Composable
   840	private fun TransformedFigmaAssetImage(
   841	    base: FigmaBounds,
   842	    target: FigmaBounds,
   843	    scale: Float,
   844	    @DrawableRes resId: Int,
   845	    alpha: Float = 1f
   846	) {
   847	    val density = LocalDensity.current.density
   848	    Image(
   849	        painter = painterResource(id = resId),
   850	        contentDescription = null,
   851	        contentScale = ContentScale.FillBounds,
   852	        modifier = Modifier
   853	            .absoluteOffset(x = figma(base.x, scale), y = figma(base.y, scale))
   854	            .requiredSize(figma(base.w, scale), figma(base.h, scale))
   855	            .graphicsLayer {
   856	                this.alpha = alpha
   857	                transformOrigin = TransformOrigin(0f, 0f)
   858	                translationX = (target.x - base.x) * scale * density
   859	                translationY = (target.y - base.y) * scale * density
   860	                scaleX = target.w / base.w
   861	                scaleY = target.h / base.h
   862	            }
   863	    )
   864	}
   865	
   866	@Composable
   867	private fun FigmaAssetImage(
   868	    x: Float,
   869	    y: Float,
   870	    w: Float,
   871	    h: Float,
   872	    scale: Float,
   873	    @DrawableRes resId: Int,
   874	    alpha: Float = 1f
   875	) {
   876	    Image(
   877	        painter = painterResource(id = resId),
   878	        contentDescription = null,
   879	        contentScale = ContentScale.FillBounds,
   880	        modifier = Modifier
   881	            .absoluteOffset(x = figma(x, scale), y = figma(y, scale))
   882	            .requiredSize(figma(w, scale), figma(h, scale))
   883	            .graphicsLayer(alpha = alpha)
   884	    )
   885	}
   886	
   887	@Composable
   888	private fun FigmaStage(background: Brush, content: @Composable BoxWithConstraintsScope.(Float) -> Unit) {
   889	    BoxWithConstraints(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
   890	        val outerMaxWidth = this.maxWidth
   891	        val outerMaxHeight = this.maxHeight
   892	        val scale = min(outerMaxWidth.value / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, outerMaxHeight.value / FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT)
   893	        val stageWidth = figma(FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, scale)
   894	        val stageHeight = figma(FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT, scale)
   895	
   896	        Box(
   897	            modifier = Modifier
   898	                .fillMaxSize()
   899	                .background(background)
   900	        ) {
   901	            BoxWithConstraints(
   902	                modifier = Modifier
   903	                    .align(Alignment.Center)
   904	                    .requiredSize(stageWidth, stageHeight)
   905	            ) {
   906	                content(scale)
   907	            }
   908	        }
   909	    }
   910	}
   911	
   912	private fun figma(px: Float, scale: Float) = (px * scale).dp
   913	
   914	private fun introBackgroundBrush(solidProgress: Float, gradientProgress: Float): Brush {
   915	    val solid = lerpColor(ColorFrame1Background, ColorFrame2Background, solidProgress)
   916	    return Brush.verticalGradient(
   917	        colors = listOf(
   918	            lerpColor(solid, ColorIntroGradientTop, gradientProgress),
   919	            lerpColor(solid, ColorIntroGradientCenter, gradientProgress),
   920	            lerpColor(solid, ColorIntroGradientBottom, gradientProgress)
   921	        )
   922	    )
   923	}
   924	
   925	private fun solidBrush(color: Color): Brush = Brush.verticalGradient(
   926	    colors = listOf(color, color)
   927	)
   928	
   929	private fun bootProgressAt(bootStartMillis: Long): Float {
   930	    val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - bootStartMillis
   931	    return (elapsed / BOOT_ANIMATION_MS.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
   932	}
   933	
   934	private fun easedSegment(value: Float, start: Float, end: Float): Float {
   935	    val progress = ((value - start) / (end - start)).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
   936	    return FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(progress)
   937	}
   938	
   939	private fun linearSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
   940	    val ms = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * BOOT_ANIMATION_MS
   941	    return ((ms - startMs) / (endMs - startMs).toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
   942	}
   943	
   944	private fun easedSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
   945	    return FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
   946	}
   947	
   948	private fun bouncySegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
   949	    return bouncyEasing(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
   950	}
   951	
   952	private fun bouncyEasing(value: Float): Float {
   953	    val t = value.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
   954	    val base = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(t)
   955	    val bounce = sin(t * PI.toFloat() * 4.5f) * (1f - t) * 0.13f
   956	    return base + bounce
   957	}
   958	
   959	private fun lerpFloat(start: Float, end: Float, amount: Float) = start + (end - start) * amount
   960	
   961	private fun lerpBounds(start: FigmaBounds, end: FigmaBounds, amount: Float) = FigmaBounds(
   962	    x = lerpFloat(start.x, end.x, amount),
   963	    y = lerpFloat(start.y, end.y, amount),
   964	    w = lerpFloat(start.w, end.w, amount),
   965	    h = lerpFloat(start.h, end.h, amount)
   966	)

    41	    AvatarTarget(CinerificProfile.Martin, Bounds(368f, 428f, 220f, 220f)),

    46	internal class CinerificIntroView(context: Context) : View(context) {
    47	    var bootStartMillis: Long = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
    48	        set(value) {
    49	            if (field == value) return
    50	            field = value
    51	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
    52	        }
    53	    var onAvatarSelected: ((CinerificProfile) -> Unit)? = null
    54	
    55	    private val bitmapOptions = BitmapFactory.Options().apply {
    56	        inScaled = false
    57	        inPreferredConfig = Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888
    58	    }
    59	    private val logoSimple = decode(R.drawable.logo_simple_large)
    60	    private val logoEyes = decode(R.drawable.logo_eyes_large)
    61	    private val logoCombined = combineBitmaps(logoSimple, logoEyes)
    62	    private val steveAvatar = circularBitmap(decode(R.drawable.steve_avatar))
    63	    private val martinAvatar = circularBitmap(decode(R.drawable.martin_avatar))
    64	    private val jannyAvatar = circularBitmap(decode(R.drawable.janny_avatar))
    65	    private val guestAvatar = circularBitmap(decode(R.drawable.guest_avatar))
    66	    private val steveName = decode(R.drawable.steve_name)
    67	    private val martinName = decode(R.drawable.martin_name)
    68	    private val jannyName = decode(R.drawable.janny_name)
    69	    private val guestName = decode(R.drawable.guest_name)
    70	
    71	    private val imagePaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG or Paint.FILTER_BITMAP_FLAG or Paint.DITHER_FLAG)
    72	    private val backgroundPaint = Paint()
    73	    private val tempRect = RectF()
    74	    private var pressedAvatarProfile: CinerificProfile? = null
    75	    private var clickAvatarProfile: CinerificProfile? = null

    77	    init {
    78	        isClickable = true
    79	        isFocusable = true

    82	    override fun onAttachedToWindow() {
    83	        super.onAttachedToWindow()
    84	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
    85	    }
    86	
    87	    override fun onDraw(canvas: Canvas) {
    88	        super.onDraw(canvas)
    89	
    90	        val progress = bootProgressAt(bootStartMillis)
    91	        val blackToBurgundy = linearSegmentMs(progress, 0, SCREEN_RED_MS)
    92	        val burgundyToSettle = easedSegmentMs(progress, 2500, 3900)
    93	        drawIntroBackground(canvas, blackToBurgundy, burgundyToSettle)
    94	
    95	        val stageScale = min(width / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, height / FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT)
    96	        val stageLeft = (width - FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stageScale) / 2f
    97	        val stageTop = (height - FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT * stageScale) / 2f
    98	
    99	        val logoAlpha = linearSegmentMs(progress, 90, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS)
   100	        if (logoAlpha > 0.01f) {
   101	            drawSettlingLogo(canvas, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, progress, logoAlpha)
   102	        }
   103	
   104	        val avatarAlpha = easedSegmentMs(progress, 2300, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
   105	        if (avatarAlpha > 0.01f) {
   106	            val y = lerpFloat(56f, 0f, avatarAlpha)
   107	            drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, steveAvatar, Bounds(130f, 428f + y, 220f, 220f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarAlpha)
   108	            drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, martinAvatar, Bounds(368f, 428f + y, 220f, 220f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarAlpha)
   109	            drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, jannyAvatar, Bounds(606f, 432f + y, 220f, 220f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarAlpha)
   110	            drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, guestAvatar, Bounds(844f, 428f + y, 220f, 220f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarAlpha)
   111	
   112	            drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, steveName, Bounds(130f, 683f + y, 220f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarAlpha)
   113	            drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, martinName, Bounds(368f, 683f + y, 220f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarAlpha)
   114	            drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, jannyName, Bounds(606f, 683f + y, 220f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarAlpha)
   115	            drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, guestName, Bounds(854f, 683f + y, 200f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarAlpha)
   116	        }
   117	
   118	        if (progress < 1f) {
   119	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   120	        }
   121	    }
   122	
   123	    override fun onTouchEvent(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
   124	        return when (event.actionMasked) {
   125	            MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN -> {
   126	                pressedAvatarProfile = settledAvatarHitProfile(event.x, event.y)
   127	                pressedAvatarProfile != null
   128	            }
   129	            MotionEvent.ACTION_UP -> {
   130	                val releasedAvatarProfile = settledAvatarHitProfile(event.x, event.y)
   131	                val shouldNavigate = pressedAvatarProfile != null && pressedAvatarProfile == releasedAvatarProfile
   132	                clickAvatarProfile = pressedAvatarProfile
   133	                pressedAvatarProfile = null
   134	                if (shouldNavigate) {
   135	                    performClick()
   136	                    true
   137	                } else {
   138	                    clickAvatarProfile = null
   139	                    false
   140	                }
   141	            }
   142	            MotionEvent.ACTION_CANCEL -> {
   143	                pressedAvatarProfile = null
   144	                clickAvatarProfile = null
   145	                false
   146	            }
   147	            else -> pressedAvatarProfile != null
   148	        }
   149	    }
   150	
   151	    override fun performClick(): Boolean {
   152	        super.performClick()
   153	        if (!isFinalFrameSettled()) return false
   154	        onAvatarSelected?.invoke(clickAvatarProfile ?: CinerificProfile.Guest)
   155	        clickAvatarProfile = null
   156	        return true
   157	    }

   159	    private fun drawSettlingLogo(
   160	        canvas: Canvas,
   161	        stageLeft: Float,
   162	        stageTop: Float,
   163	        stageScale: Float,
   164	        progress: Float,
   165	        alpha: Float
   166	    ) {
   167	        val logoEntry = bouncySegmentMs(progress, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS, LOGO_ENTRY_END_MS)
   168	        val finalProgress = easedSegmentMs(progress, FINAL_SETTLE_START_MS, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
   169	        val base = Bounds(222f, 150f, 750f, 535f)
   170	        val final = Bounds(297f, 11f, 600f, 428f)
   171	
   172	        if (logoEntry >= 0.999f) {
   173	            drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, logoCombined, lerpBounds(base, final, finalProgress), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
   174	            return
   175	        }
   176	
   177	        val simpleEntry = lerpBounds(Bounds(447f, 1037f, 300f, 214f), base, logoEntry)
   178	        val eyesEntry = lerpBounds(Bounds(453f, -417f, 300f, 214f), base, logoEntry)
   179	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, logoSimple, lerpBounds(simpleEntry, final, finalProgress), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
   180	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, logoEyes, lerpBounds(eyesEntry, final, finalProgress), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
   181	    }
   182	
   183	    private fun drawIntroBackground(canvas: Canvas, solidProgress: Float, gradientProgress: Float) {
   184	        if (gradientProgress <= 0.001f) {
   185	            canvas.drawColor(lerpColor(COLOR_FRAME_1, COLOR_FRAME_2, solidProgress))
   186	            return
   187	        }
   188	
   189	        val solid = lerpColor(COLOR_FRAME_1, COLOR_FRAME_2, solidProgress)
   190	        backgroundPaint.shader = LinearGradient(
   191	            0f,
   192	            0f,
   193	            0f,
   194	            height.toFloat(),
   195	            intArrayOf(
   196	                lerpColor(solid, COLOR_GRADIENT_TOP, gradientProgress),
   197	                lerpColor(solid, COLOR_GRADIENT_CENTER, gradientProgress),
   198	                lerpColor(solid, COLOR_GRADIENT_BOTTOM, gradientProgress)
   199	            ),
   200	            null,
   201	            Shader.TileMode.CLAMP
   202	        )
   203	        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), backgroundPaint)
   204	        backgroundPaint.shader = null
   205	    }
   206	
   207	    private fun drawFigmaBitmap(
   208	        canvas: Canvas,
   209	        bitmap: Bitmap,
   210	        bounds: Bounds,
   211	        stageLeft: Float,
   212	        stageTop: Float,
   213	        stageScale: Float,
   214	        alpha: Float
   215	    ) {
   216	        tempRect.set(
   217	            stageLeft + bounds.x * stageScale,
   218	            stageTop + bounds.y * stageScale,
   219	            stageLeft + (bounds.x + bounds.w) * stageScale,
   220	            stageTop + (bounds.y + bounds.h) * stageScale
   221	        )
   222	        imagePaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
   223	        canvas.drawBitmap(bitmap, null, tempRect, imagePaint)
   224	        imagePaint.alpha = 255
   225	    }
   226	
   227	    private fun decode(resId: Int): Bitmap {
   228	        return BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, resId, bitmapOptions)
   229	    }
   230	
   231	    private fun isFinalFrameSettled(): Boolean {
   232	        return bootProgressAt(bootStartMillis) >= 1f
   233	    }
   234	
   235	    private fun settledAvatarHitProfile(x: Float, y: Float): CinerificProfile? {
   236	        if (!isFinalFrameSettled() || width <= 0 || height <= 0) return null
   237	
   238	        val stageScale = min(width / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, height / FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT)
   239	        val stageLeft = (width - FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stageScale) / 2f
   240	        val stageTop = (height - FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT * stageScale) / 2f
   241	
   242	        return FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS.firstOrNull { target ->
   243	            val bounds = target.bounds
   244	            val centerX = stageLeft + (bounds.x + bounds.w / 2f) * stageScale
   245	            val centerY = stageTop + (bounds.y + bounds.h / 2f) * stageScale
   246	            val radius = min(bounds.w, bounds.h) * stageScale / 2f
   247	            val dx = x - centerX
   248	            val dy = y - centerY
   249	            dx * dx + dy * dy <= radius * radius
   250	        }?.profile
   251	    }
   252	}
   253	
   254	private data class AvatarTarget(
   255	    val profile: CinerificProfile,
   256	    val bounds: Bounds
   257	)
   258	
   259	private data class Bounds(
   260	    val x: Float,
   261	    val y: Float,
   262	    val w: Float,
   263	    val h: Float
   264	)
   265	
   266	private fun bootProgressAt(bootStartMillis: Long): Float {
   267	    val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - bootStartMillis
   268	    return (elapsed / BOOT_ANIMATION_MS.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
   269	}
   270	
   271	private fun linearSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
   272	    val ms = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * BOOT_ANIMATION_MS
   273	    return ((ms - startMs) / (endMs - startMs).toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
   274	}
   275	
   276	private fun easedSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
   277	    return FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
   278	}
   279	
   280	private fun bouncySegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
   281	    return bouncyEasing(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
   282	}
   283	
   284	private fun bouncyEasing(value: Float): Float {
   285	    val t = value.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
   286	    val base = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(t)
   287	    val bounce = sin(t * PI.toFloat() * 4.5f) * (1f - t) * 0.13f
   288	    return base + bounce
   289	}
   290	
   291	private fun lerpFloat(start: Float, end: Float, amount: Float) = start + (end - start) * amount
   292	
   293	private fun lerpBounds(start: Bounds, end: Bounds, amount: Float) = Bounds(
   294	    x = lerpFloat(start.x, end.x, amount),
   295	    y = lerpFloat(start.y, end.y, amount),
   296	    w = lerpFloat(start.w, end.w, amount),
   297	    h = lerpFloat(start.h, end.h, amount)
   298	)
   299	
   300	private fun lerpColor(start: Int, end: Int, amount: Float): Int {
   301	    val t = amount.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
   302	    return Color.argb(
   303	        lerpInt(Color.alpha(start), Color.alpha(end), t),
   304	        lerpInt(Color.red(start), Color.red(end), t),
   305	        lerpInt(Color.green(start), Color.green(end), t),
   306	        lerpInt(Color.blue(start), Color.blue(end), t)
   307	    )
   308	}
   309	
   310	private fun lerpInt(start: Int, end: Int, amount: Float): Int {
   311	    return (start + (end - start) * amount).roundToInt()
   312	}
   313	
   314	private fun combineBitmaps(base: Bitmap, overlay: Bitmap): Bitmap {
   315	    val output = Bitmap.createBitmap(base.width, base.height, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
   316	    val canvas = Canvas(output)
   317	    val paint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG or Paint.FILTER_BITMAP_FLAG or Paint.DITHER_FLAG)
   318	    canvas.drawBitmap(base, 0f, 0f, paint)
   319	    canvas.drawBitmap(overlay, 0f, 0f, paint)
   320	    return output
   321	}
   322	
   323	private fun circularBitmap(source: Bitmap): Bitmap {
   324	    val output = Bitmap.createBitmap(source.width, source.height, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
   325	    val canvas = Canvas(output)
   326	    val rect = RectF(0f, 0f, source.width.toFloat(), source.height.toFloat())
   327	    val maskPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG)
   328	    val imagePaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG or Paint.FILTER_BITMAP_FLAG or Paint.DITHER_FLAG)
   329	    canvas.drawOval(rect, maskPaint)
   330	    imagePaint.xfermode = PorterDuffXfermode(PorterDuff.Mode.SRC_IN)
   331	    canvas.drawBitmap(source, null, rect, imagePaint)
   332	    imagePaint.xfermode = null
   333	    return output
   334	}

============================================================
FILE: README.md
============================================================

     1	# Cinerific
     2	
     3	Local Android project for the `prahlin/cinerific` GitHub repo.
     4	
     5	This is a first Kotlin / Jetpack Compose scaffold based on the Figma intro and sign-in frames. The current app opens as a tablet-friendly full-screen frame preview with:
     6	
     7	- Intro frame 1
     8	- Animated intro frame 2
     9	- Promo/logo intro frame 3
    10	- Sign-in/loading frame 4
    11	
    12	## Stack
    13	
    14	- Kotlin
    15	- Jetpack Compose Material 3
    16	- Android Gradle Plugin 8.5.x
    17	- Gradle 8.7
    18	
    19	## Run it
    20	
    21	Open the project in Android Studio and run `app`, or use the Gradle wrapper:
    22	
    23	```bash
    24	./gradlew :app:assembleDebug
    25	```
    26	
    27	The project pins Gradle to Android Studio's bundled JBR in `gradle.properties`, so command-line builds do not accidentally use an unsupported system JDK.
    28	
    29	From VS Code, run the default build task `Android: assemble debug`. There are also tasks for `Android: install debug` and `Android: launch Cinerific` when an emulator or device is connected.
    30	
    31	## Notes for the first Kotlin pass
    32	
    33	- The layout is intentionally simple and adaptive for tablets.
    34	- The current visuals are Compose approximations of the Figma frames and are meant to be replaced with production assets screen by screen.
    35	- The local project folder is the same path as the GitHub remote: `prahlin/cinerific`.

     1	plugins {
     2	    id("com.android.application") version "8.5.2" apply false
     3	    id("org.jetbrains.kotlin.android") version "1.9.24" apply false
     4	}

FILE: app/build.gradle.kts

     1	plugins {

============================================================
FILE: read_project.sh
============================================================

     1	#!/bin/bash
     2	
     3	PROJECT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
     4	OUTPUT_FILE="$PROJECT_DIR/cinerific_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt"
     5	
     6	echo "Generating Cinerific project snapshot..."
     7	echo "Project directory: $PROJECT_DIR"
     8	echo "Snapshot: $OUTPUT_FILE"
     9	echo ""
    10	
    11	write_header() {
    12	  cat > "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
    13	Cinerific Android PROJECT SNAPSHOT
    14	Generated: $(date)
    15	Project directory: $PROJECT_DIR
    16	
    17	IMPORTANT INSTRUCTIONS FOR CHATGPT & CODEX:
    18	1. Read this entire snapshot before giving code edits.
    19	2. Preserve all assets, spacing, alignment, fonts, images, layout, animations, and relative positioning unless specifically asked to change them.
    20	3. Do not remove unrelated code.
    21	4. Do not rewrite large sections unless requested.
    22	5. When giving code changes, include exact line/row numbers whenever possible.
    23	6. Print full scripts when requested.
    24	7. Treat this project structure as the source of truth.
    25	8. If you don't understand something, ask for clarification instead of making assumptions.
    26	9. Warn me, or at least caution me, if I ask for something that would break the project or cause it to not work as intended.
    27	
    28	
    29	============================================================
    30	SNAPSHOT IGNORE POLICY
    31	============================================================
    32	
    33	This file is rebuilt from the current Cinerific workspace every time ./read_project.sh runs.
    34	Ignored paths are omitted from the project tree and file dumps unless they appear inside an included text file such as .gitignore or read_project.sh.
    35	
    36	The snapshot intentionally ignores:
    37	- .git
    38	- .gradle
    39	- .idea
    40	- app/build
    41	- build
    42	- .kotlin
    43	- .tmp
    44	- .cache
    45	- .DS_Store
    46	- local.properties
    47	- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
    48	- *.jks
    49	- *.keystore
    50	
    51	Binary image and screenshot files are inventoried by path, size, and modified time only. Raw binary bytes are not printed.
    52	
    53	
    54	============================================================
    55	PROJECT FILE TREE
    56	============================================================
    57	
    58	EOF
    59	}
    60	
    61	print_file() {
    62	  local file="$1"
    63	
    64	  if [ -f "$file" ]; then
    65	    {
    66	      echo ""
    67	      echo ""
    68	      echo "============================================================"
    69	      echo "FILE: ${file#$PROJECT_DIR/}"
    70	      echo "============================================================"
    71	      echo ""
    72	      nl -ba "$file"
    73	    } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    74	  fi
    75	}
    76	
    77	print_binary_inventory() {
    78	  local title="$1"
    79	  local dir="$2"
    80	
    81	  {
    82	    echo ""
    83	    echo ""
    84	    echo "============================================================"
    85	    echo "$title"
    86	    echo "============================================================"
    87	    echo ""
    88	  } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    89	
    90	  if [ ! -d "$dir" ]; then
    91	    echo "Directory not present: ${dir#$PROJECT_DIR/}" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    92	    return
    93	  fi
    94	
    95	  find "$dir" -type f \
    96	    ! -name ".DS_Store" \
    97	    ! -name "*_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt" \
    98	    ! -name "*.jks" \
    99	    ! -name "*.keystore" \
   100	    | sort | while IFS= read -r file; do
   101	    local size
   102	    local modified
   103	    size="$(stat -f "%z" "$file" 2>/dev/null || stat -c "%s" "$file" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")"
   104	    modified="$(stat -f "%Sm" -t "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S" "$file" 2>/dev/null || stat -c "%y" "$file" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")"
   105	    printf '%s | %s bytes | modified %s\n' "${file#$PROJECT_DIR/}" "$size" "$modified" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   106	  done
   107	}
   108	
   109	write_snapshot() {
   110	  write_header
   111	
   112	  find "$PROJECT_DIR" \
   113	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.git" -prune -o \
   114	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.gradle" -prune -o \
   115	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.idea" -prune -o \
   116	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/app/build" -prune -o \
   117	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/build" -prune -o \
   118	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.kotlin" -prune -o \
   119	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.tmp" -prune -o \
   120	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.cache" -prune -o \
   121	    -name ".DS_Store" -prune -o \
   122	    -name "local.properties" -prune -o \
   123	    -name "*_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt" -prune -o \
   124	    -name "*.jks" -prune -o \
   125	    -name "*.keystore" -prune -o \
   126	    -print | sort >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   127	
   128	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   129	
   130	
   131	============================================================
   132	FILE CONTENTS — ANDROID SOURCE
   133	============================================================
   134	
   135	EOF
   136	
   137	  for file in \
   138	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml" \
   139	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/MainActivity.kt" \
   140	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/ui/CinerificApp.kt" \
   141	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/ui/CinerificIntroView.kt" \
   142	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/ui/theme/Theme.kt" \
   143	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/values/strings.xml" \
   144	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/values/themes.xml" \
   145	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/values-night/themes.xml" \
   146	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/values-v31/themes.xml" \
   147	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/drawable/splash_transparent.xml" \
   148	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/drawable/ic_launcher_background.xml" \
   149	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/drawable/ic_launcher_foreground.xml" \
   150	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/mipmap-anydpi-v26/ic_launcher.xml" \
   151	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/mipmap-anydpi-v26/ic_launcher_round.xml"
   152	  do
   153	    print_file "$file"
   154	  done
   155	
   156	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   157	
   158	
   159	============================================================
   160	FILE CONTENTS — ROOT / TOOLING
   161	============================================================
   162	
   163	EOF
   164	
   165	  for file in \
   166	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.gitignore" \
   167	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.github/copilot-instructions.md" \
   168	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.vscode/mcp.json" \
   169	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.vscode/tasks.json" \
   170	    "$PROJECT_DIR/README.md" \
   171	    "$PROJECT_DIR/build.gradle.kts" \
   172	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/build.gradle.kts" \
   173	    "$PROJECT_DIR/settings.gradle.kts" \
   174	    "$PROJECT_DIR/gradle.properties" \
   175	    "$PROJECT_DIR/gradle/wrapper/gradle-wrapper.properties" \
   176	    "$PROJECT_DIR/read_project.sh"
   177	  do
   178	    print_file "$file"
   179	  done
   180	
   181	  print_binary_inventory "BINARY / IMAGE ASSET INVENTORY — DRAWABLE-NODPI" "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/drawable-nodpi"
   182	  print_binary_inventory "BINARY / SCREENSHOT INVENTORY" "$PROJECT_DIR/screenshots"
   183	
   184	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   185	
   186	
   187	============================================================
   188	NOT INCLUDED IN SNAPSHOT
   189	============================================================
   190	
   191	The Cinerific snapshot intentionally excludes generated/cache/runtime/private directories:
   192	- .git
   193	- .gradle
   194	- .idea
   195	- app/build
   196	- build
   197	- .kotlin
   198	- .tmp
   199	- .cache
   200	- .DS_Store
   201	- local.properties
   202	- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
   203	- *.jks
   204	- *.keystore
   205	
   206	The snapshot lists binary assets and screenshots by path, size, and modified time, but does not print raw PNG/JPG/PDF bytes.
   207	
   208	EOF
   209	}
   210	
   211	write_snapshot
   212	
   213	echo "Done."
   214	echo ""
   215	echo "Snapshot created here:"
   216	echo "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   217	echo ""
   218	echo "Open it with:"
   219	echo "open \"$OUTPUT_FILE\""

============================================================
NOT INCLUDED IN SNAPSHOT
============================================================

The Cinerific snapshot intentionally excludes generated/cache/runtime/private directories:
- .git
- .gradle
- .idea
- app/build
- build
- .kotlin
- .tmp
- .cache
- .DS_Store
- local.properties
- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
- *.jks
- *.keystore

The snapshot lists binary assets and screenshots by path, size, and modified time, but does not print raw PNG/JPG/PDF bytes.
